Document 001: 10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ 政策金利の２％超えは？

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 14 日(水)

1/3 
10 月の金融政策決定会合は利上げ見送りへ  
政策金利の２％超えは？ 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月30日の金融政策決定会合に先立って、早くも観測記事が金融市場を賑わせている。米系
通信は「日銀は今月会合で利上げ見送る公算大、12月の可能性排除せず」と報じた。記事内
では「経済・物価は日銀の見通しに沿って推移しており、２会合連続での利上げが必要なほ
ど物価の上振れリスクが一段と強まっている情勢にはない」、とはいえ「一時的な要因を除
いた基調的な物価上昇率は上昇を続けており、目標の２％から上振れていくリスクには依然
として注意が必要だ」などとする日銀関係者の発言が記述されていた。また既報のとおり、
基調的物価上昇率が概ね２％に達したとの判断を展望レポートに記すことにも触れられてい
た。

・10月の利上げ見送りの根拠として、昨日発表された９月の企業物価指数は一定の存在感があ
る。国内企業物価指数は前月比▲0.3％、前年比＋6.8％と上昇が一服し、市場予想の同＋
7.6％も下回った。原油価格が高止まりするなか、世界的なＡＩ・半導体関連需要の拡大など
を背景に非鉄金属や化学製品などで上昇が続いた一方、米を中心とする農林水産物の価格下
落が下押しに寄与した。円ベースの輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.5％と大幅な上昇を記録した
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
が、８月の同＋25.1％からは小幅に鈍化した。川上段階のインフレ圧力は依然として強いも
のの、９月会合時点の判断に大きな修正を迫るものではなかったと考えられる。

・同日発表された「生活意識に関するアンケート調査」（９月調査）も似たような結果であっ
た。５年後の物価予想については「かなり上がる」の割合が51.1％と前回調査対比で概ね横
ばいとなり、「少し上がる」との合計では86.5％と高止まりした。他方、今後５年間の物価
上昇率（年平均）は、中央値が５％で横ばいも、平均値は＋10.1％と前回調査対比で0.7％pt
低下した。ガソリン代の補助に加え、米価の下落が効いたとみられ、家計の予想物価上昇率
が一段と上放れする事態は回避された。政府による家計支援策が効いているとはいえ、家計
の予想物価上昇率が低下したことは、日銀に時間的余裕を与え得る。なお、景況感について
は「１年前との比較」と「１年後の予想」の双方とも大きな変化は認められなかった。

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
・先行きの金融政策はどうなるであろうか。筆者は、10月会合は利上げ見送り、12月会合で利
上げを決定し、政策金利は1.5％で越年すると予想する。Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測が燻り、円安圧
力が強いなか、日銀が金融引き締め方向への手を緩めるとなれば、一段と円安が進行しかね
ない。また日銀短観（９月調査）を見る限り、企業の金融環境に大きな変化はみられず、既
往の利上げが実体経済に染み渡っている様子はそれほど窺えない。これらを踏まえ、筆者は
2027年も３～４回の利上げがあり、政策金利は２％を超えることを想定している。

・ただし、Ｆｅｄが2027年に政策金利を据え置き、その次の政策変更が利下げになるとの見方
が生じれば、日銀は円安対策から解放され、利上げを中断ないしは終了する可能性がある。
その点、ウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁とウォラー理事の発言は注視しておきたい。まずウィ
リアムズ総裁は10月13日に「来年はインフレがかなり鈍化し、通年でみれば、全体のインフ
レ率は目標⁠の２％近辺になるとみている。これは私が言及した要因、すなわち関税の影響が
完全に過去のものとなることを反映している」として、2027年に利上げは必要ないとの見方
を示した。

・⁠ウォラー理事は10月13日に「最近の米国債利回りの上昇は経済成長の加速とＡＩへの大規模
投資によるもので、インフレ期待の高まりが原因ではない」、「インフレ期待は引き続き
『しっかりと安定している』」と述べ、その上で「データセンター建設を含むＡＩ投資が経
済の需要を支えており、今後２～３年はその効果が続く⁠可能性がある。大幅な生産性向上が
実現するのは、なお『数年先』だ」とした。同理事の発言を整理すると、①予想インフレは
安定しており、その点において現在の金融政策は適切である、②ＡＩ投資が一巡すれば、そ
れに起因するインフレ圧力は鈍化する、③その後に生産性向上によるディスインフレ方向へ
の動きが予想される、といったところであろう。

・両氏の予想が現実のものになれば、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測は後退し、場合によっては2027年に
利下げ観測が台頭することもあり得る。そうした環境下、日銀は利上げペースを緩める、或
いは終了を検討するなど、多くの選択肢を手にすることができる。

世界経済：AI関連需要の広がりが世界景気をけん引。企業の景況感は堅調に推移
 製造業は節目50を上回る良好な水準を維持。非製造業は金融・消費関連を中心に加速
― 製造業はAI関連投資がけん引し、防衛支出の拡大も寄与。一方、中東情勢を受けた在庫積み増しの寄与は縮小傾向
― 非製造業は金融サービスやAI関連需要がけん引。エネルギー高で落ち込んでいた観光・娯楽も回復
 国・地域別では、米国では非製造業が大幅に改善。日本やユーロ圏では製造業を中心に改善

― 日本ではAI・半導体需要と外需拡大がけん引し、ユーロ圏は電気・電子部品を含む中間財の生産が好調。AI投資ブー
ムの広がりがうかがわれる
1

### Page 3

金融市場概況：原油高を背景に日米金利が上昇
 日米株価は、9月下旬にハイテク株を中心に上昇
― 9月上旬は、日米株価とも軟調。原油高を背景とした日米金利上昇が重荷に
― 下旬に入ると、米AI関連株が買い戻されたことからナスダックが上昇に転じ、日経平均もつれ高の展開に
 ドル円は一時円高が進展したものの、日米金利差が拡大し、再度150円台後半まで円安が進む「往って来い」の展開に
― 日本・米国とも利上げを実施。先行きも利上げ継続見込みだが、米金利上昇幅がより大きく、日米金利差は拡大
― 日銀は物価上振れリスクに対応し、利上げペースを加速へ。次回利上げは26年12月会合と予想

米国：個人消費は一時的に減速も、サービス中心に底堅さ維持
 7月実質個人消費支出は前月比＋0.0％と減速も、一時要因による下押しが大きく、消費の基調は崩れていないと評価
― 例年より前倒しで6月に実施された大手通販セールの反動から、財消費が減少
― 一方、そうした影響を受けづらいサービス消費については強さを維持。高齢化を背景に医療支出が拡大したほか、W杯
開催等を受け、娯楽サービス、外食・宿泊、輸送サービスなどの選択的支出も増加
 小売販売の高頻度データは、足元で伸びが再び高まっており、7月の減速が一時的であったことを示唆

米国：所得減速下でも株高による資産効果が消費を下支え
 消費が底堅い背景に、富裕層を中心とした株価上昇による資産効果の持続
― 株高を受けてインフレ下でも家計の実質金融資産は着実に増加。可処分所得対比でも高水準を維持。実質所得が弱含
む中でも、金融資産の増加が家計の購買力を下支え
 先行きは物価鈍化に伴う実質所得の改善も消費の追い風に
― 実質可処分所得は前年比＋0.5％と伸び悩みが継続。先行きは物価鈍化を受けて実質所得も伸び幅を徐々に持ち直す
見込み

### Page 6

米国：雇用は予想外の大幅増。一時的要因の影響を受けつつも労働市場の底堅さを確認
 8月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋16.2万人と市場予想（同＋5.6万人）を大幅に上回る結果
― 過去2カ月も合わせて5.5万人の上方修正。速報段階で減少した7月もプラス転化
― 娯楽・宿泊（同＋6.2万人）や教育・医療（同＋2.8万人）を中心に民間就業者が伸びを高めたほか、政府部門も同＋3.5
万人増と押し上げに寄与
― 政府部門では、バックトゥスクールに伴い教員、スクールバス運転士、カフェテリアスタッフなどの雇用が増加（例年みら
れる季節的な動きであるが、統計上の調整にゆがみが生じている可能性）

失業率は低位で横ばい
低失業率の一因に「少ない供給」
米国：失業率は低位安定。厳格化する移民政策が労働供給を一段と下押し
 失業率は4.1％と前月からほぼ横ばい。引き続きFOMC参加者が見込む長期均衡水準（Longer run）を下回る低水準
 低失業率の一因に労働参加率の低下。シニア層の退職増加に加え、移民政策の厳格化の影響も大
― 米移民・関税執行局（ICE）の摘発増など移民取り締まりが強化されるなか、メキシコ出身者などの労働人口は減少傾向
― 足元ではハイチなど複数国の一時保護資格（TPS、※）が相次ぎ終了。今後もエルサルバドルなどで有効期限を迎え、7

月以降の累計で最大100万人程度がTPSに基づく就労資格を失う可能性。労働供給を更に押し下げる要因に
（※）紛争や災害等により安全な帰国が困難な特定国の出身者に対し、一時的な在留・就労を認める制度
6
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：対中赤字拡大への危機感を背景に、EUは厳格な域内生産要件を定めたIAA修正案を公表
11
 対中貿易赤字の拡大を背景に、EUの貿易収支は悪化傾向
― 工業製品の対中輸入増加を受け、EUは2024年以降、EV関税等の措置を講じるも歯止めがかからず
 こうした中、欧州議会の3共同報告者はIAA（産業加速法）の修正案を公表。域内生産品の優先（Made in EU要件）等の面
で、欧州委員会原案に比べ要件を厳格化する方向。日本を含む域外国のEU域内での生産・投資に影響を与えるおそれ
―Trusted Partner要件には再輸出・第三国企業子会社等を通じた迂回リスクの排除に言及あり。域外国はサプライチェー
ンの見直しを迫られる可能性がある他、要件適合を確認する事務コスト発生の懸念も

 インフレ長期化の蓋然性が高まるなか、ECBは預金ファシリティ金利を2.50％に引き上げ

日本：半導体製造装置を中心としたAI関連財にけん引され、生産は緩やかな増勢が継続
15
 鉱工業生産は7月も緩やかな増勢が継続。AI関連財の増産がけん引役に
―AI関連財は2025年9月比＋11.3％と大幅増産。半導体製造装置を中心に半導体検査機器やDC関連財にも裾野が拡大

― 令和8年熊本地震で減産が懸念された自動車や電子部品・デバイスも増産。7月時点で生産下押しは限定的との評価
 先行きの生産も、AI関連財主導で緩やかな増勢が続くと予想
―8月生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（補正値ベース）と堅調。半導体製造装置などの増産が寄与するとの見立て
― 地震による供給制約長期化の懸念は小。自動車や電子部品・デバイスの8月予測修正率はプラスで、増産継続見込み

日本：耐久財特需一服後も、所得改善を背景に緩やかな回復基調を維持
16
 耐久財特需の押し上げは一服
― 自動車・エアコン購入の特需は徐々に剥落。食品などの非耐久財は根強い節約志向を背景に弱含み
― 一方、サービス消費は増加基調を維持。個人消費は一時的な上振れから元の緩やかな拡大トレンドへ回帰する動き
 個人消費は良好な所得環境を背景に今後も緩やかな回復基調で推移する見通し
― 実質賃金の伸びは低下も、夏季賞与要因の剥落が原因。実質所定内給与は足元でも1％程度の伸びを維持
― 懸念材料は再び上昇し始めた原油価格。高インフレが再燃すれば、個人消費の回復に水を差す懸念

（注） 旅行収支要因は、非居住者による国内消費（インバウンド消費）を控除し、居住者に
よる海外消費（アウトバウンド消費）を加えるための調整要因。アウトバウンド消費
はSNAの家計最終消費支出に含まれるが、GDPの計算上は輸入として控除される

日本：日銀追加利上げは最短インターバル。今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げへ
17
 日銀は9月会合で利上げを決定（反対2名）。利上げは6月以来3カ月ぶりで、今次局面では最も短いインターバル
―9月会合後の会見で植田総裁は、物価押し上げから物価上振れ抑制へと「政策の局面が変化した」と説明
― 政策金利は1.25％と、日銀による中立金利推計値のレンジ下限を上回るが、金融環境は利上げ後も緩和的と評価
 今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げを続ける可能性大
― 大幅利上げや連続利上げについては物価上振れリスクが大きい場合の選択肢と説明。一方、急激な政策変更を回避す

る観点から、先手を打って利上げを実施していく姿勢
物価上振れリスクへの対応から
利上げ間隔が短期化
政策金利とブレークイーブン・インフレ
（注） ブレークイーブン・インフレは基調的物価上昇率の一面を捉えていると考えられるが、
必ずしも基調的物価上昇率そのものを示す指標ではない点に留意
（出所）日本銀行、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
日銀9月会合のポイント（声明文・総裁会見）
声明文
• 企業の賃金・価格設定行動の積極化や中長期的な予想物価上昇
率の上昇などを踏まえると、2％物価目標を上振れるリスクがある
• 企業間取引における価格上昇圧力が消費者段階にも波及し始め
ているほか、賃金上昇の販売価格への転嫁の動きも続いている
• 政策金利の変更後も、緩和的な金融環境は維持される
会見での注目発言
• 基調的物価上昇率が2％に接近し、政策局面が変化。物価上振れ
を回避する観点が重要に
• 原油価格の上昇やそこから波及した物価上昇圧力、AI関連需要

や円安進行が幅広い品目の物価上昇圧力に繋がる可能性
• 基調的物価の2％定着を確認するのにはしばらく時間がかかる
• 金融緩和の度合いは利上げにより少しずつ縮まっているが、銀行
貸し出しや資産市場は依然として緩和的
• 急激な利上げで金融環境が大きく変化することや、それによる経
済へのマイナス影響を回避する観点は重要
• 大幅利上げに追い込まれる可能性を減らすように政策を運営する

米国債券市場：インフレ懸念と追加利上げ観測を背景に利回りは上昇
 9月の米10年国債利回りは、インフレ懸念の高まりから5.2％まで上昇
― 月前半は、雇用統計の上振れや原油高、物価指標の高止まりから、長期金利は5％の大台に
― 中旬は、FOMCが25bpの利上げを決定。追加利上げの可能性も示唆され、長期金利は5％前後で高止まり
― 月後半は、堅調な景況感やFRB高官のタカ派発言を背景に、長期金利は5.2％台まで上昇
 今後は、ホルムズ海峡を巡る中東情勢の動向が鍵に。原油高が続けば、複数回の追加利上げにも現実味
― トランプ大統領がイランの提示した停戦案を拒否するなど、中東情勢の先行きは依然として不透明

2026 年獲利亮眼，惟 2027 年供給壓力漸增 
 歐美航線運價優於預期，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 19.62 元：3Q26 營收
1,467.76 億元(+51.4%Y oY)，優於預期。受惠運價上漲獲利率提升，
然考量燃油成本占整體營業成本逾 2 成，油價上漲仍對成本形成壓力，
預估稅後淨利 424.74 億元(+95.3%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 19.62 元。 
 受地緣政治及供應鏈重組影響，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.24 元：展
望後市，根據 Clarksons Aug -26 預測，2026 年全球貨櫃供給年增
4.6%，需求年增 3.8%，供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年
逐步收斂。預估 2026 年營收 4,735.4 億元(+24.9%Y oY)，稅後淨利
991.6 億元(+44.6%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 45.8 元。展望 2027 年，隨新船
陸續交付，儘管塞港情況仍可能限縮有效 運力，惟考量整體供給成長
仍高於需求，預期市場運價將逐步回歸常態。整體而言，預估2027 年
營收 4,097.3 億元(-13.5%YoY)，稅後淨利 482.4 億元(-51.4%Y oY)，
稅後 EPS 22.28 元。 
 維持中立評等，目標價 265 元：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 元計算，
目前 PB 0.8X ，介於長榮疫情前區間 0.5-1.5X 中下緣，儘符合高股息
題 材 ， 然 隨 歐 美 傳 統 淡 季 到 來 ， 維 持 中 立 評 等 ， 目 標 價265 元
(2Q27BVPS PB 0.9X)。

個股報告 
長榮 為 全 球 貨 櫃 運 力 第七大 航
商，市占率 5.9% 
 長榮成立於 1968 年 9 月，實收資本額 216.5 億元。截至 3Q26，全球超過 1.3
萬位員工及 293 個代理行，航線擴及 101 個國家，總船隊數共 242 艘(自有船
比例超過 6 成)，預計至 2030 年仍有 72 艘船待交付。根據 Alphaliner 統計，
截至目前長榮為全球貨櫃運力第七大航商，市佔率約 5.9%。長榮 1H26 營收
結構為貨運收入 93%，船艙租收入 1.2%，其他 5.8%。

歐 美 航 線 運 價 優 於 預 期 ， 預 估
3Q26 稅後 EPS 19.62 元 
 長榮 Sep-26 營收 497.11 億元(+1.48%MoM，+63.75%Y oY)，3Q26 營收達成
率 105.5%，優於預期，主要受惠歐美航線運價走升，且旺季附加費及燃油附
加費挹注營收 。獲利方面 ，受惠運價上漲帶動獲利率提升 ， 惟中東衝突於Jul-26
一度趨緩後再度升溫，且考量燃油成本占整體營業成本逾 2 成，油價上漲仍對
成本形成壓力，預估 3Q26 毛利率 38.91%。由於營收規模擴大，費用率有望
下降 ，預估營益率 35.71%，假設 匯率 持穩 ，預估稅後淨利 424.74 億元
(+164.9%QoQ，+95.3%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 19.62 元。

歐美航線需求放緩 ，預估 4Q26
稅後 EPS 14.89 元 
 根據上海航交所 10/9 公告，上海出口集裝箱運價指數 (SCFI) 3,664.11 點
(+0.05%WoW，+215.76%Y oY)，隨中國十一長假結束，歐線運價短期反彈，
分別為遠東-歐洲線+3.41%WoW，遠東-地中海線+0.2%WoW；美線持平，遠
東-美西線+0.08%WoW，遠東-美東-0.04%WoW。觀察 10/11 公告跨大西洋、
歐 洲 及 跨 太 平 洋 航 線 運 力 分 別 變 動 -1.14%WoW 、 -1.88%WoW 及
+0.48%WoW。整體而言，隨歐美航線逐步進入傳統淡季，航商陸續調整運力
配置，其中歐洲線運力投放減少，美線則大致持平，顯示航商持續透過運力調
節支撐市場運價。

展望 4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期後
續運價將呈緩步回落走勢。美線方面，旺季需求已近尾聲，且部分船班裝載率
已有下滑，儘管 Sep-26 川習會後美國調降部分消費品關稅，包括玩具、小型
家電及節慶裝飾品等，惟目前尚未出現明顯大型備貨潮。供給面而言，隨水情
陸續改善 ， 巴拿馬運河管理局預計自2026/10/15 起將每日允許通行量由 32 艘
回升至 33 艘，惟仍低於原先 36 艘水準。考量大型貨櫃船通行具一定優先性，
目前對船期營運影響相對有限，惟通行量尚未完全恢復，仍可能使美東線有效
運力有所收斂。另，美伊衝突及紅海通行風險仍存， Gemini 聯盟持續恢復部
分紅海航線，近期亦傳出 Premier Alliance 有望於 4Q26 恢復部分紅海航線，
若後續其他航商陸續跟進，繞航所吸收之有效運力恐逐步釋放，對市場運價形
成壓力。

整體而言，儘管近洋線進入傳統旺季，惟長榮歐美航線營收占比較高，預期整
體運價及貨量將隨淡季到來而回落 ， 預估4Q26 營收 1,350.87 億元(-8%QoQ，
+57.6%Y oY)。獲利方面，儘管油價仍維持高位，惟隨貨量減少，相關變動成
本亦有望下降，預估毛利率 37.9%。另考量長榮往年於 4Q 提列年終獎金費
用，預期費用率將季增，預估營益率 29.4%。假設匯率持穩，預估稅後淨利
323.53 億元(-23.8%QoQ，+279.8%Y oY)，4Q26 稅後 EPS 14.94 元。

2026 年有效運力供給受限 ， 預估
稅後 EPS 45.75 元 
 展望後市，根據 Clarksons Aug-26 預測，2026 年全球貨櫃供給年增 4.6%，需
求年增 3.8%，供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年逐步收斂。短期而
言，受到缺工及天候因素影響，部分港口作業效率下降，近期塞港現象增加，
有助吸收部分有效運力。展望 2027 年，在尚未考慮紅海全面復航之前提下，
Clarksons 預估全球貨櫃供給+8.8%Y oY，需求+3.5%YoY，供需缺口將再度擴
大；若紅海航線進一步恢復，繞航所吸收之有效運力亦將逐步釋放，供給壓力
恐進一步增加。需求方面，則須持續關注停戰協議進展、美國關稅政策變化及
通膨壓力對終端需求之影響。

展望 2026 年，長榮持續優化航線布局，強化東南亞、中東、非洲及中南美洲
等區域配置 ， 並透過長約布局降低市場運價波動影響，整體而言 ，元富預估 2026
年營收 4,735.4 億元(+24.9%Y oY)。獲利方面，預估毛利率 30.8%，營益率
25.4%，稅後淨利 991.6 億元(+44.6%Y oY)，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 45.8 元。
展望 2027 年，隨新船陸續交付，全球貨櫃船隊運力持續增加，儘管塞港情況
仍可能限縮有效運力，惟考量整體供給成長仍高於需求，預期市場運價將逐步
回歸常態。整體而言，預估 2027 年營收 4,097.3 億元(-13.5%Y oY)，稅後淨利
482.4 億元(-51.4%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 22.28 元。

### Page 1

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
Recharging Growth: UPS Recovery and HVDC Potential; Initiate at Buy
CITI'S TAKE 
We initiate coverage on Voltronic with a Buy rating and NT$1,050 target 
price. Voltronic is a Taiwan-based UPS ODM, supplying to western power 
vendors including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton. We view 2026E as the 
earnings trough, with improving online UPS mix and operating leverage 
driving 28% earnings rebound in 2027E. Structural outsourcing by western 
power vendors should support sustained share gains, while expansion into 
HVDC power racks offers a new stream for AIDC growth, supporting a 
potential re-rating. Our TP applies 25x 2027E EPS against earnings CAGR 
of 20% in 2026-2028E, broadly consistent with its L-T historical average.
Outsourcing and mix upgrade underpin the recovery — Western vendors’ 
increasing focus on integrated AIDC infrastructure strengthens the rationale for 
outsourcing select UPS platforms. Voltronic’s customization capabilities and 
flexible manufacturing position it to capture additional opportunities. A rising 
contribution from online and higher power UPS should support margins, driving EPS 
CAGR of 20% in 2026-2028E. 
Power racks could materially broaden its growth opportunity — Voltronic’s high 
voltage EV-charger experience and longstanding relationships with western power 
vendors support its entry into HVDC power solutions. It has progressed into 
customer sampling, with management indicating that HVDC power rack mass 
production could begin in 2H27E at the earliest. Successful commercialization could 
broaden its addressable market and support a valuation re-rating.
Earnings recovery and strong cash returns  — We view 2026E as the earnings 
trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. A richer online UPS mix and operating 
leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and 
2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash position and 
high dividend payout should provide additional support for shareholder returns.
Initiate Buy with TP NT$1,050 on 25x 2027E EPS — Our target multiple is broadly 
consistent with Voltronic’s average forward P/E since 2013, supported by its 
competitive position in UPS outsourcing and durable customer relationships. 
Clearer evidence of power-rack commercialization could offer further re-rating. 
n
Angela Hsu AC

### Page 4

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Executive Summary
We initiate coverage of Voltronic with a Buy rating and target price of NT$1,050
(25x 2027E EPS), supported by structural UPS outsourcing, improving product mix
and emerging opportunities in AI datacenter power infrastructure. Voltronic is a
Taiwan-based ODM of uninterrupted power supplies (“UPS”) and power
conversion products for global brands. UPS system provides backup electricity and

protection against power disturbances, helping critical equipment maintain
operation during supply disruptions. UPS accounted for 63% of Voltronic’s 2025
group sales. Its pure-play ODM model, customization capabilities and
longstanding customer relationships underpin its competitive positioning.
Our positive stance on the shares is based on three major reasons:
1. We see expansion into HVDC power racks as a potentially significant new
growth opportunity. Its experience in high-voltage EV chargers provides

relevant engineering capabilities, while its established relationships with
western power vendors support access to customer development programs.
Voltronic has progressed into customer sampling, with management
indicating that HVDC power rack mass production could begin in 2H27E at the
earliest. Successful commercialization could broaden its addressable market
and support a valuation re-rating, although meaningful earnings contribution
remains subject to qualification, design wins and production schedules.

2. Structural UPS outsourcing should support sustained market share gains.
Western power vendors are allocating more resources to integrated AIDC
infrastructure and energy management, strengthening the rationale for
outsourcing selected products. Voltronic’s engineering expertise, flexible
manufacturing and ability to handle a broad range of customized products in
small batches make it well positioned to capture these opportunities.
3. The shift toward online and higher power UPS should improve revenue quality

and profitability. We expect online UPS to be the principal driver of the
earnings recovery, with online UPS sales growing 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E as
outsourcing opportunities and demand for higher power systems expand.
We view 2026E as the earnings trough, followed by a recovery in 2027-2028E. We
forecast EPS of NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E, implying
earnings growth of 28% and 12% in 2027E and 2028E. A richer online UPS mix and

operating leverage should lift GPM from 27.1% in 2026E to 28.1% and 29.0% in
2027E and 2028E, respectively. Its relatively asset-light business model, net cash
position and high dividend payout should provide additional support for
shareholder returns.
Our target price applies 25x forward P/E to 2027E EPS, broadly consistent with
Voltronic’s long tern average forward P/E since 2013. We believe its competitive
position in UPS outsourcing, durable customer relationships and manufacturing

capabilities justify this multiple, with clearer evidence of power rack
commercialization offering potential for further re-rating.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)

Investment Thesis
Positive #1 – AIDC creates a new growth runway, with
power-rack expansion potentially lifting Voltronic into
broader power solutions
We see Voltronic’s expansion into power racks as a potentially significant extension
of its AIDC opportunity in the medium to long term, enabling the company to
capture the broader share of datacenter power spending. We believe Voltronic’s
existing high-voltage engineering capabilities, established relationships with

western power vendors and customer collaboration provide a credible foundation
for winning business in HVDC power rack.
Rapid growth in AI workloads and rising rack density increase the power capacity
required, alongside more demanding requirements for efficiency, reliability and
load management. Nvidia’s roadmap targets 800VDC infrastructure supporting
racks of 1MW and beyond, while its latest architecture includes power racks that
supply 800VDC to compute racks within existing AC-powered facilities. We believe

this creates scope for greater power-infrastructure, even as the architecture
consolidates conversion stages to improve efficiency.
EV-charger experience provides a relevant technical foundation for HVDC
development
Voltronic already offers DC fast chargers ranging 30-240kW, with modular designs
delivering constant power across a 300-100VDC range and charging controls
developed internally. These products demonstrate existing experience with high-

voltage conversion, protection, thermal management and modular power-system
design. We believe these capabilities should support its development of 800VDC
PSUs and reduce the engineering learning curve.
Established westerns customer relationships strengthen its route to market
Voltronic’s UPS ODM business provides existing engineering relationships and
familiarity with customers’ quality, customization and manufacturing
requirements. Its pure ODM business model also emphasizes avoiding competition

with customers. We believe this positioning makes it a suitable partner for western
vendors who are expanding their AI infrastructure offerings. Existing relationships
should help Voltronic secure development opportunities and understand product
roadmaps early, although each new platform still requires qualification.
Voltronic has already taken concrete steps toward this opportunity. The company
has publicly introduced an HVDC PSU for power racks, while its broader

development plan extends into solutions combining battery backup unit (BBUs).
battery management systems (BMS), and DCDC converters through collaboration
with power-system companies.
Management indicated discussions with two Tier 1 vendors with the intended
solution ultimately servicing the service providers. This creates a potential path
from supplying individual power products to participating in a larger, integrated
power-rack offering.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 9

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Development has progressed into customer sampling, although specifications
continue to evolve with customers’ requirements. Management pointed out that
shipment of 800VDC products possible from 2H27E at the earliest.
We view its existing relationships with global power-solution vendors as a key
advantage, while its commitment to operating as a pure ODM without competing
against customers under its own brand supports this partnership model.

That said, customer qualification, confirmed design wins and mass-production
timing remain key milestones before we can confidently evaluate a material
contribution. Sales potential also depends on Voltronic’s scope of supply, which
determines content per rack and the share of system value it captures. Progress
toward commercial shipments could strengthen earnings visibility and support a
re-rating as Voltronic establishes its position in AIDC power infrastructure.

Positive #2 – UPS outsourcing provides a structural share-
gain opportunity

We see structural outsourcing as a durable growth driver for Voltronic, allowing its
UPS business to outgrow underlying industry demand. Rising investment in digital
infrastructure, commercial applications and industrial automation supports
continued demand for power protection, while established brands have incentives
to outsource selected hardware platforms.
Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency position it to capture this opportunity. In our view, growth can come from

both an expanding market and the transfer of existing production from customers’
factories to qualified manufacturing partners.
UPS remains a structurally growing power-protection market
Global Market Insights estimates the worldwide UPS market at US$13bn in 2025,
rising to US$14.5bn in 2026E and US$26.8bn by 2035E, implying a 7.1% CAGR
over 2026-2035E. Although estimates vary by research provider and market
definition, the underlying drivers include increasing dependency on electronic

systems, growth in industrial automation and smart manufacturing, increasing
power outages and grad instability, rising costs of downtime and greater
requirements for reliable electricity.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 11

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
11
Datacenters provide an important source of incremental demand, while
commercial and industrial applications broaden the market beyond the AI
investment cycle. Datacenters expansion supports demand for online, double
conversion UPS systems, which continuously condition incoming power and
protect critical equipment against supply disturbance. Commercial applications –
including offices, retail networks and healthcare facilities – require protection for

servers, communications equipment and other essential systems. Industrial
demand comes from protecting sensitive control, automation equipment and
critical processes where power interruptions can disrupt production.
The competitive landscape is led by established international suppliers. Per Global
Market Insights 2025 analysis, Schneider Electric, Eaton, Vertiv are the three
leading suppliers, with the top 5 collectively controlling approximately 60-65% of

the global UPS market. Other peers include Huawei, KSTAR, Kehua, Delta and
Riello.
This concentration matters for the outsourcing thesis – securing additional projects
from a relatively small number of large brands can open substantial manufacturing
opportunities.

Western brands’ strategic priorities support further selective outsourcing

Per our industry checks, major UPS brands still manufacture a substantial portion
of their UPS products internally, while increasingly moving toward energy
management and committing resources to broader AIDC infrastructure. This could
result in small and medium UPS products to be gradually outsourced as this
transition develops. We view this as a multi-year opportunity, particularly where
established platforms can be transferred to qualified ODMs while customers retain

control over product specifications, branding and distribution.
We identify three economic reasons for this shift –
1.Outsourcing can improve manufacturing and capital efficiency: A specialist
ODM can aggregate demand across customers, consolidate component
purchases and spread manufacturing overhead across a larger production
base. For brands, this can reduce incremental factory investment and simplify
capacity planning.
2.Outsourcing can accelerate product development: access to ODMs’

established platforms, component knowledge and manufacturing processes
allows brands to adapt existing designs for additional markets and
applications. Engineering resources can then concentrate on product
differentiation.
3.Broader system offerings create competing demands on vendors’ resources.
Leading UPS brands emphasize on monitoring, operational management and
integrated solutions, strengthening the rationale for outsourcing selected
hardware while allocating more resources to system integration, and software.

Voltronic’s dedicated ODM model, engineering capabilities and manufacturing
efficiency should position it to capture further UPS outsourcing

Voltronic’s ODM focus is evident from its commitment to operate without having a
brand of its own to compete with its customers. This aligns its interests with
customers and differentiates it from suppliers that combine branded sales with
contract manufacturing. Long-standing relationships with leading vendors,
including Schneider, Riello, Vertiv and Eaton, provide a foundation for securing

additional projects. Its engineering capabilities enable it to meet diverse product
specifications, while flexible manufacturing supports the small production batches
and broad model range characteristic of the UPS industry.
Its track record in product qualification and certification also creates barriers, as
moving established programs to other suppliers requires additional validation,
audits and testing. We believe this combination of customer trust, execution and

cost competitiveness positions Voltronic to secure more outsourcing orders,
including higher-power online UPS, as western brands allocate greater resources
to AIDC infrastructure and integrated power solutions.

Positive #3 – Online and high-power UPS mix upgrade
should support structurally better growth
We expect Voltronic’s shift toward online and higher-power UPS to become an
increasingly important driver of revenue growth and profitability. Moving up the

product range increases power capacity and engineering content per system,
supporting higher ASPs and greater differentiation. We forecast online UPS sales to
rise at a 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, ahead of offline UPS at 6%, with online UPS
gross profit growing faster at 16% as product mix improves.
Offline UPS remains a stable revenue base, yet offers more limited differentiation.
Offline UPS typically serves home offices, small businesses and basic power

applications. These products feature relatively simple designs and lower costs.
We view this segment as increasingly mature and commoditized, with lower
technical barriers exposing suppliers to greater pricing pressure, including
competition from Chinese manufacturers. Scale, procurement efficiency and cost
control hence are the key competitive factors, while opportunities for sustained
ASP and margin expansion are more constrained, in our view. Our model assumes

offline UPS gross margin to be at 24-26% in 2025-2028E.
Online UPS offers greater technical differentiation and stronger margin potential.
Its double-conversion architecture continuously converts AC power into DC and
back into AC, providing stable voltage and frequency, avoiding power disturbances
and enabling zero transfer time to battery operation.
It is particularly suited to complex, power-sensitive applications such as
datacenters, industrial automation and precision control systems, where even brief

interruptions or power quality disturbances can disrupt operations. The business
also exhibits high-mix, low-volume characteristics, particularly in customized
Schneider, 18%
Vertiv, 5%
Riello, 5%

Others, 70%

### Page 15

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
15
commercial and industrial applications, where customers require different power
ratings, redundancy configuration and battery interfaces etc. Our model assumes
online UPS gross margin to be at 31-34% in 2025-2028E.
We believe this creates opportunities for an experienced ODM partner such as
Voltronic to differentiate through customization and reliable delivery, while sharing
development and procurement resources across customers and product platforms.

T ogether with demanding certification, customer qualification and reliability
testing, these capabilities raise entry barriers and switching costs, supporting
higher ASPs, strong customer retention and better margin potential as Voltronic
expands into higher power systems.
We project online UPS sales to increase 12% CAGR in 2026-2028E, lifting its
contribution to group sales from 37% in 2025 to c.44% in 2028E. The underlying
driver comes from a stronger outsourcing opportunity thanks to the AIDC boom.

Western vendors, including Schneider, Vertiv are expanding their focus on
integrated AIDC infrastructure, power distribution, and infrastructure
management. Meanwhile, rising power requirements within conventional AC-
based datacenters support demand for higher power UPS systems, prompting
vendors to expand UPS production capacity. We believe the combination of
stronger demand and competing resource requirements can strain available
engineering and manufacturing capabilities, increasing the appeal of qualified

ODM partners to supplement internal production. Voltronic’s customization
capabilities and flexible manufacturing position it to capture this opportunity.

### Page 17

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
17
Financial Analysis
Income statement – 2026E marks the earnings trough; online UPS drives
the recovery from 2027E
Voltronic delivered an 8.7% sales CAGR and 9.9% net profit CAGR over 2018-2025,
although earnings have become more volatile in recent years due largely to swings
in the inverter business. We forecast sales to decrease 1% y/y to NT$20.4bn in
2026E, before recovering 8% y/y and 7% y/y to NT$21.9bn and NT$23.4bn in

2027E and 2028E respectively. The key driver is the higher value online UPS.
We forecast online UPS sales to grow 10%/13%/10% y/y in 2026E/2027E/2028E,
lifting its sales contribution from 37% in 2025 to 44% by 2028E. T ogether with
offline UPS, we estimate UPS products to make up 72% of group sales in 2028E vs
2025’s 63%. Conversely, we forecast inverter sales to decline 27% y/y in 2026E and
remain broadly flat thereafter, reducing their sales contribution from 29% in 2025

to 18% in 2028E.
We expect gross margin to bottom at 27.1% in 2026E vs. 29.0% in 2025, reflecting
negative impact from material cost inflation and weaker inverter profitability.
Thereafter, we forecast gross margin to recover to 28.1% and 29.0% in 2027E and
2028E, supported by a richer online UPS mix and improving segment margins. We
estimate online UPS gross margin to increase from 30.8% in 2026E to 33.4% in
2028E driven rising contribution from high power online UPS, while its rising sales

mix could provide increasing contribution to group gross profits.
Operating leverage should further support profitability. Opex fell from 8.0% of sales
in 2025 to 7.4% in 2026E and could remain at 7.2% thereafter, while R&D
investment is maintained at 4.2% of sales. We therefore forecast OPM to recover
from 19.7% in 2026E to 20.9% and 21.8% in 2027E and 2028E.
We estimate net profit to decrease 18% y/y in 2026E, partly hampered by a larger
FX loss, before rebounding 28% y/y and 12% y/y in 2027E and 2028E. We forecast

EPS to register NT$33.00/NT$42.30/NT$47.25 in 2026E/2027E/2028E.
We attribute Voltronic’s earnings decline in 2025 and our forecast for a further
decline in 2026E to persistent inverter weakness and additional cost and supply
pressures. In 2025, adverse weather in Pakistan shortened the inverter peak
season and weakened demand, while intensifying Chinese competition pressured
pricing and profitability. The weakness extended into 2026E as the Middle East

conflict disrupted logistics and delayed shipments, alongside continued customers
destocking in Pakistan. In 1H26, RMB appreciation further weighed on margins.
Meanwhile, raw material shortages constrained shipments and higher metal and
key component prices eroded gross margins. We therefore forecast earnings to
decline 18% y/y in 2026E before improving 28% y/y in 2027E.

Balance Sheet – Asset light model and net cash suggest high returns
Voltronic maintains a relatively asset-light balance sheet with fixed assets making
up 24% of total assets in 2025 despite its sizable sales scale. We estimate the

company to remain in a net cash position throughout 2026-2028E. We forecast
cash and cash equivalent assets to account for 36-39% of total assets in 2026-
2028E while there are no meaningful short-term borrowings and long-term debt.
We forecast ROE to rise from 37% in 2025 to 40% in 2026E and 48% in 2027E. The
high ROE is not driven by financial leverage, but primarily by its asset-light
business model, strong operating margins and high capital distribution.

Voltronic also maintains high shareholder-return policy. We assume an 87% cash
dividend payout ratio through 2026-2028E, implying cash dividends of
NT$29/NT$37/NT$41 per share.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1,050, broadly in line with its long-term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s established
earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating leverage to its
profitability. Using 2027E EPS captures a fuller year of anticipated earnings

recovery following the softer 2026E base, alongside the ongoing transition toward
online and higher power UPS. This also aligns our valuation with the earnings
outlook investors are likely to focus on as the recovery progresses.
Our target multiple of 25x is supported by Voltronic’s competitive position in UPS
outsourcing. Its established relationship with western power vendors and ability to
accommodate high mix, low volume production creates a solid foundation for the

intact share gains, supporting the durability of its earnings and hence justify a
valuation broadly consistent with its historical average. Stronger evidence of power
rack commercialization and sustained UPS mix improvement could support a
further re-rating.

Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include:
1.Slower UPS demand or outsourcing gain. Weaker commercial and industrial
spending, delayed datacenter projects or customer inventory adjustments
could weigh on orders.
2.  Less favorable product mix and margin pressure. A slower transition toward
online and high-power UPS, more aggressive pricing or higher component
costs could limit margin improvement.

3. Persistent PV inverter competition. Aggressive pricing by Chinese suppliers,
weaker residential solar demand or unfavorable subsidy changes could
pressure inverter sales and profitability.
Our quantitative model assigns a High Risk rating to Voltronic. However, we
believe such a rating is not warranted due to: 1) its established franchise and
longstanding relationships with leading power vendors, with demanding
qualification requirements and high customization supporting customer retention

and repeat business; 2) its track record of profitability and positive operating cash
flow enables internally funded investment and reduces reliance on external
financing; and 3) the company has a strong balance sheet and has been in net cash
position.

### Page 24

Voltronic Power (6409.TW)
13 October 2026 Citi Research
24
Company Overview
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014, Voltronic
Power is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. The company is headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing
facilities in Taiwan, China and Vietnam, with approximately 5,000 employees.

Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model and does not market products
under its own brand. This avoids direct competition with customers and has helped
the company establish long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS
brands, as well as numerous regional brand owners.
The company provides customers with product design, R&D, sourcing,
manufacturing and system integration services, with a focus on high-mix,
relatively low-volume and customized products.

Voltronic’s core products include offline UPS systems ranging from 400VA to 3kVA
and on-line UPS systems ranging from 1kVA to 3MVA. The company has
progressively expanded from conventional single phase UPS products into three-
phase, high-power and modular UPS systems. It has also diversified into off-grid
and hybrid solar inverters, energy storage systems, and EV chargers.
In 2025, online UPS contributed 37% of group sales, offline UPS 26%, inverters

29% and others 8%. By geo, Asia accounted for 60% of group sales, followed by
Americas 22%, EU 16% and others 2%.

Shareholder Structure
As of March 2026, founder, chairman and general manager Alex Hsieh was the

largest individual shareholder, holding 9.6% of outstanding shares. Based on
disclosed direct, family-related and investment company holdings, we estimate
the founder and related parties collectively controlled c.30% of outstanding
shares.
Key Management Team
nAlex Hsieh – Founder, Chairman and General Manager: Mr. Hsieh has led
Voltronic since its establishment in 2008. He holds a bachelor’s degree in
business management from Tatung University and previously served as

Chairman and General Manager of Centralion Industrial and as a director of
Phoenixtec Power. His extensive experience in the UPS industry underpins
Voltronic’s pure-play ODM strategy.
nMonica Wang – Vice General Manager: Ms. Wang joined Voltronic at its
establishment in 2008. She previously worked as a manager at Centralion
Industrial.
nChih-Hsin Chin – Vice General Manager: Mr. Chin joined Voltronic in 2008.
Before joining the company, he worked as a manager at Phoenixtec Power.

Voltronic Power
Company description
Established in 2008 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2014,
Voltronic is a Taiwan-based design and manufacturing service (DMS) provider
specializing in uninterrupted power supplies (UPS) and power-conversion
products. Voltronic adopts a pure-play ODM business model, which avoids
direct competition with customers and has helped the company establish

long-term relationships with most global Tier-1 UPS brands. The company is
headquartered in Taipei and operates manufacturing facilities in Taiwan,
China and Vietnam.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Voltronic, supported by an earnings recovery,
structural UPS outsourcing and emerging opportunities in AIDC power
infrastructure. Its customization capabilities and longstanding relationships
with western power vendors position it to capture additional outsourcing

orders and expand into higher power products. HVDC power racks offer a L-T
growth opportunity and potential re-rating catalyst. We view 2026E as
earnings trough, with a richer online UPS mix and operating leverage driving a
28% earnings rebound in 2027E.
Valuation
We value Voltronic using a forward target P/E of 25x our 2027E EPS to derive a
target price of NT$1050, broadly in line with its long term average forward P/E
since 2013. We believe forward P/E is appropriate given the company’s

established earnings base and the importance of product mix and operating
leverage to its profitability.
Risks
Key downside risks to our Buy rating and target price include: 1) Slower UPS
demand or outsourcing gain. 2) Less favorable product mix and margin
pressure. 3) Persistent PV inverter competition. 4) Weaker macro economy

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

ASE Technology Holding

3Q26 sales outperformance affirms solid 
transformation progress

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 900.00 TWD | Price: 762.00 TWD

3Q26 sales beat on LEAP ramp and mature ASP strength 
ASE reported 3Q26 sales +28% QoQ better than its guide, driven by stronger IC ATM 
(+20% QoQ vs. BofAe +13% QoQ) and mild upside in EMS (+42% QoQ vs. BofAe +40% 
QoQ). We believe IC ATM strength is led by modest mainstream packaging and testing 
price hike on industry supply tightness, supplemented by LEAP business ramp. EMS also 
benefited from Apple product seasonal builds and higher component cost pass through. 
With improving packaging and testing utilization and ASP and rising LEAP mix, we 
expect its IC ATM GMs to improve 300bps QoQ to 30% (vs. high-20% guide) though 
consolidated GMs (+180bps QoQ to 23%) could be partially offset by EMS outgrowth. 
AI pipeline diversification anchors capex plan in ’26-28 
We model ASE’s 4Q26 IC ATM sales +7-9% QoQ as its LEAP opportunity diversifying 
from TSMC outsourcing to direct project wins including AMD’s Venice CPU, putting sales 
on track to +42% YoY in ’26 (vs. its +35% guide). Its IC ATM GMs could further expand 
to 31% as LEAP reaching 30% of its sales (vs. 18%/22% in 1H/3Q26). While we expect 
ASE to spend US$13bn+ annual capex in ’27-28 (vs. US$10.5bn in ’26), the accelerating 
IC ATM sales (BofAe +40% CAGR from ’26-28) on advanced expansion and mature price 
uplift, along with the business mix upgrade to cloud AI/HPC projects carrying 35-45% 
GMs should offset hiking depreciation (BofAe +39% CAGR from ’26-28), lifting its IC 
ATM GM from 29% in ’26 to low 30% in ’27 and mid 30% in ’28 with operating leverage. 
Stronger earnings quality unlocks re-rating potential 
We lift our ’26/27/28E EPS to NT$20/NT$34/NT$50 on strong LEAP outlook. Keep Buy 
rating and raise POs from NT$800 (US$51) to NT$900 (US$58) still on 20x 2H27-1H28 
P/E (vs. its 5-26x range) backed by its structural business transformation with better 
sales and GMs growth profile as the industry supply further consolidates amid higher 
tech entry barrier.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Established in 1984 and headquartered in Taiwan, ASE 
Technology Holding is the no.1 outsourced assembly and 
testing (OSAT) firm globally, in terms of market share by 
revenue. ASE acquired Siliconware Precision (SPIL) in 2018, 
and had acquired USI in 2010, which became its EMS 
business segment providing synergies in system-level 
packaging. 2 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
We rate ASE Buy, factoring in the company's opportunity in 
advanced packaging and testing evidenced in TSMC's 
outsourcing and its own heavy capex investment. The 
strategy shift should allow the company to capture fast 
growing addressable market in high performance 
computing applications and improves its margins. ASE 
could maintain its leading position in the Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) universe, owing 
to its well-established footprints and widespread service 
offerings.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
ASE Technology Holding (XSRIF / ASX) 
We value ASE Technology Holding at NT$900 per share (US$58 for ADR), based on 20x 
2H27-1H28E P/E, above its historical range of 5-26x, in view of its solidified industry 
position in AI era. We use P/E ratio to value ASE as the company has been able to 
maintain healthy profitability through the cycles in the past 20 years due to 
consolidating industry landscape supporting a more benign supply/demand and keeping 
pricing and margins less cyclical.

Downside risks to our PO are 1) share loss and/or ASP erosion owing to Chinese players' 
cannibalization and/or ASE's worsening execution, 2) weakening end demand due to 
uncontrollable matters, such as macro halt and/or geopolitical tensions, 3) regulatory 
issues that could hinder ASE from generating synergies and benefit its competitors.

### Page 1

Nien Made Enterprise

3Q preview: EPS +13% YoY to NT$6.6; 
Better 2H but not yet a turnaround

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 441.00 TWD | Price: 358.50 TWD

3Qe EPS NT$6.6: strong rev and GP offset by opex and FX 
Nien Made is scheduled to report 3Q26 results on 4 Nov. Disclosed monthly sales imply 
3Q26 revenue growth of 16% YoY to NT$8.5bn, or 9% in USD terms, largely 
volume-driven. Growth reaccelerated from 1.7% YoY in 1H26, led by custom-made on a 
low US base and expansion in Europe and Japan. Ready-made also improved as 
Cambodia labor shortages eased. (See Exhibit 1 for US window coverings spending 
trends). Gross margin: We expect underlying GPM to rise 1.1ppt YoY to 59.8% on lower 
tariffs, broadly flat QoQ. We forecasts an NT$300mn of IEEPA tariff refunds in 2H26. 
Assuming NT$150mn is booked in 3Q, this would add 1.7ppt and lift reported GPM to 
61.5%. We expect the selling expense ratio to fall 1.7ppt QoQ to 17.5% on lower fuel 
charges, but remain 0.5ppt higher YoY due to continued salesforce and distribution 
network investment. We expect an FX loss in 3Q26 vs. an NT$118mn gain in 3Q25, 
weighing on YoY earnings growth. We forecast 3Q26 EPS of NT$6.6, up 13% YoY. 
US window covering picking up, but no turnaround yet  
BofA credit/debit card data suggests US spending on window coverings turned positive 
in Aug-26 for the first time after 19 consecutive months of YoY declines. Spending rose 
4%/3% YoY in Aug/Sep, respectively. This improvement partly explains the recent 
reacceleration in Nien Made's monthly sales. That said, we view the improvement more 
as normalization off a low base than the start of an industry turnaround. The Aug-Sep 
2025 comparison base was relatively weak, while the current rate-hike environment 
remains a headwind to housing-related demand. 
Slightly raise FY26 EPS est. to NT$22.9; PO to NT$441 
We raise our FY26 EPS estimate by 2% to NT$22.9 to reflect the blended impact of 
stronger-than-expected revenue growth and FX loss. We maintain our FY26 DPS forecast 
of NT$17 per share. We raise our PO by 4% to NT$441, as we roll forward our valuation 
basis from FY26E EPS to FY27E EPS. At the same time, we lower our target P/E from 
19x to 17x, reflecting the MSCI removal and a less supportive rate environment.

Company Sector 
Interior Furnishings

Company Description 
Nien Made, founded in 1974, is one of the world's largest 
window-covering manufacturers. It has roughly 50% 
market share in both ready-made (OEM business) shades 
and blinds, and custom-made (own brand) shutters in the 
US. The US market accounted for over 75% of sales. Key 
clients include Home Depot and Wal-Mart for ready-made 
shades and blinds. Nien Made operates its own retail 
network and e-commerce business for its custom-made 
shutters under the Norman brand name in the US and 
Europe.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on Nien Made, mainly given 1) clear 
custom-made growth profile (share gain in the US and fast-
growing DTC in Japan and Europe), 2) its largest customer 
Home Depot is gaining market share, and 3) margin 
expansion potentials backed by favorable products mix.

Price objective basis & risk 
Nien Made Enterprise (XNRBF) 
Our PO NT$441 is derived from 17x P/E 2027E EPS. This is around 0.5SD above 
historical the one-year forward P/E, which we think is warranted given the visibility of 
the growth profile.

Downside risks are 1) weaker-than-expected Home Depot's sell-through, 2) slow 
recovery of US housing turnover, and 3) higher tariff increases on China exports to the 
US. 4) Loss of key customers.

重點摘要： 
 統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率
躍升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。
2Q26 受惠生產效率提升、產能滿載毛利率躍升。 
 AI 伺服器帶動統新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要
骨幹濾片，光纖波段分離與光纖傳輸頻寬增加，會需要統新薄
膜濾光片作為訊號傳輸強化。除 Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分
散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，
2026~2031 年每年維持 31%年複合成長。 
 1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD，會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光
片。

一. 統新 2Q26 營收與財報優於市場預期，3Q26 續強 
統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率躍
升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。主要受
惠於 AI 資料中心互連需求，使得統新產線維持滿載。展望 3Q26
預估統新營收為 2.42 億元，+16.35%QoQ，毛利率 56.58%，稅後淨
利 1.06 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.76 元。統新預計產能增加 30%，以
因應客戶需求。

二. 2026 年 DWDM 隨 AI 骨幹網絡建置，濾片量增價漲 
統新高密度波長多工器( D W D M，D e n s e  W a v e l e n g t h  D i v i s i o n  
Multiplexing)，至少可傳輸 16 種不同顏色的光纖，最高可傳輸 128
種不同顏色之光纖，同一條主幹網路上，可以傳輸的頻寬因此加
大，相當具有傳輸效率。DWDM 於發送端將不同波長的光信號組
合起來(覆用, MUX)，並耦合到光纜線路上的同一根光纖中，進行
傳輸，在接收端又將多個波長的光信號分開(解覆用, DEMUX)，並
作進一步處理，恢復出原波長信號後送入不同的終端接收器。統
新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要骨幹濾片，波段分
離 ( S -B a n d、C - B a n d 傳 輸 核 心、L -B a nd 容 量 擴 充、S -B a nd 未 來 預 留、
U-Band 8nm 用於 OTDR 光纖監控偵測主幹網路是否具有斷點或需
要救援)，統新以單通道 100GHz 去細分 DWDM 濾光片，統新推出
新品，50GHz 間隔濾光片，支援的通道數量提升至 96Channels。

三.  AI 伺服器高速傳輸，帶動光通訊主被動元件量增 
Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光
通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，2026~2031 年，DWDM 市場每年維持 31%
年 複合 成長。此 外，受惠 AI 伺服 器傳 輸速 率提升，如：Broadcom、
Marvell 等國際晶片廠商，於 2026 年起已推出 800G /1.6T Switch 晶
片組，等同於 AI 伺服器後端 Ports 支援的光纖通訊傳輸會一併升
級至 800G 與 1.6T 傳輸速率。Yole 報告指出，1.6T 光纖收發模組
出貨量，2026 年/2027 年/2028 年預估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M，
1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年內呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD ， 會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光片。
在薄膜濾光片鍍膜層數 ， Scale-Across 與 NPO 所需薄膜濾光片層數
約 150L~200L，未來發展至 CPO 時鍍膜層數會再增加 8L，統新為
此預計多準備 10 條產線預計開發 250L+規格，予台系知名晶圓代
工廠商。統新指出，Scale-Out 濾片鍍膜代工量增加，是替中系系
統廠代工(層數約 100~150L)。Scale-Across 濾片鍍膜層數會大於
200L，甚至高達 250L，目前訂單能見度高，涵蓋 1.)同業產能難以
支應，Cohr 交給統新、2.)中系系統廠商加單、3.)台系網通廠商於
低軌衛星鍍膜(LEO 非光學鍍膜) 。統新毛利率之所以跳升，是先前
客戶給統新的試產量僅 20~30 片，2Q26 起量產量增至 K 級別，出
貨量增加、規模經濟效應之下，單月營收損平點(評估為 0.4~0.5
億元)，2Q26 起月營收顯著跳升至 0.6~0.7 億元。預期此營運動能
至少能見度到 4Q26，但由於 DWDM 交期延長至 6~8 個月，預估
2027 年產業供需偏向緊俏。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 375 元 
統新競爭優勢在於其生產高品質高效率之薄膜濾光片篩選特定輻
射波段的光學元件，能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動
截面，藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中 
傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高光纖的使用效率，生產良率逐步提
升且具佔有一定之市場地位。預估統新 2026 年營收 8.56 億元，
+75.77%YoY，毛利率 53.43%，稅後淨利 3.32 億元，稅後 EPS 為
+8.63 元。展望 2027 年，隨產能積極擴充之下，預估統新年營收
為 17.02 億元，+98.83%YoY ，毛利率預估為 54.85% ，稅後淨利 5.57
億元，稅後 EPS+14.47 元。考量基礎建設帶動 DWDM 規格升級，
AI 伺服器傳輸速率提升因應 1.6T 傳輸薄膜濾光片用量同步增長，
給予統新投資建議買進，目標價 375 元(26.00 X2027 EPS)。

一.公司簡介

統新(6426)為生產精密光學元件，以及高密度分波多工器(DWDM)
關鍵元件─薄膜濾光片(TFF)之專業廠商。各產品線營收占比為主
動光學元件佔比低於 15%、被動光學元件：80~85%、光學鍍膜元
件與代工低於 10%。主要產品 DWDM 濾光片 (Dense Wavelength 
Division Multiplexer，即為高密度波長多工分工器)技術可以大幅提
高光纖通訊系統之頻寬，成為光纖通訊系統的市場中關注的焦
點。統新國內主要競爭對手為東典光電科技(股)，國外廠商包含
II-IV Cohernet(美國)、Iridian Technologies(加拿大)等廠商投入相關
產品研發及生產。

圖一、光通訊元件

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二.26 年營運概況及 26 年展望 
1.統新 DWDM 技術層層堆疊良率優異  
統新主要產品為薄膜濾光片即為篩選特定輻射波段的光學元件，
能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動截面，藉由不同光線
原理而選擇通過的光源(紅、藍、綠光) ，且藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技
術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高
光纖的使用效率。

2.因應 AI 資料中心基建商機，薄膜濾光片產品走向更高規  
回顧 5G 時期的薄膜濾光片，支援頻段增加，切割更細，中心波

波長 6 組延伸至 12 組 ， 每一道光波段間距縮短至 3.5nm 甚至更

切分愈細，波段之間間距愈來愈短，需要用的薄膜濾波片數量倍
增，且每一片薄膜濾波片設計難度提升，包含設計層數 200 層以
上以及過濾效果較佳，而統新於濾波片設計良率優於競爭同業，
可望取得一定市場地位。

제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: searching for signal
By Sian Fenner, Ryan Wells & Illiana Jain
13:33 October 02 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, this week’s main event was the RBA Monetary Policy Board’s (MPB) decision to raise the
cash rate by 25bps to 4.60%. This outcome was universally expected given Governor Bullock had
already communicated that upside risks to inflation had materialised – the conditionality established by

the MPB back in August for considering another rate hike.

### Page 2

Governor Bullock made explicit note of three upside risks. The Middle East conflict was emphasised as
the risk that had “materialised”, given the re-escalation and renewed surge in crude oil and refined
product prices over recent months. The RBA’s liaison warns this could lead to further pass-through of
costs and more persistent inflation pressures. On top of this, domestic capacity pressures and the data
centre/AI investment boom were also noted as upside risks that the MPB remain alert to.

In a video update mid-week, Chief Economist Luci Ellis explains why the bar for a follow-up rate hike in
November is low. We now expect a 25bp increase in November, provided there is no sudden resolution
of the Middle East conflict beforehand, or some other event that significantly lowers the outlook for
energy-related costs.
Subsequently, the August CPI revealed that underlying (trimmed mean) inflation rose 0.2% (3.4%yr),
which was slightly below the market’s expectation but spot on Westpac’s forecast. As usual, the

monthly detail was mixed, but the key drivers of underlying inflation remain centred on housing-related
components, particularly rents and new dwelling purchases. Headline inflation remained volatile, with
the unwind of the fuel excise cut causing another spike in fuel prices. We have maintained our forecast
for a 0.9% lift in trimmed mean inflation in Q3, and with upside risks building for Q4, a follow-up rate
hike in November appears most likely.

Regarding the current growth pulse, household spending surprised with a flat reading in August.
Services spend fell back following recent strength that was driven by major sporting events, but this
was offset by an increase in goods spend, particularly in fuel and electric vehicles. Together with our
latest card data for early September, the consumer spending pulse is shading a touch softer. However,
another solid outcome for business investment could once again be on the cards for Q3. August’s

goods trade data showcased another significant spike in data centre-related equipment imports, up
$4.3bn, similar in scale to Q1. While clearly import-driven, our modelling suggests that the local
economic impact of such investment is likely to be around half of the total investment.
Before moving offshore, a final note on housing. Cotality’s latest update revealed that dwelling prices
fell another 1.1% nationally in September. While Sydney and Melbourne have led the correction to

date, the fact that the other capitals are also reporting price declines clearly indicates that the
correction is deepening and broadening, as higher interest rates collide with the tax policy changes
announced in May’s budget. Sentiment effects indeed remain at play, as evinced by the continued pull-
back in investor credit growth. Westpac anticipates a peak-to-trough decline of around 7%, with tight
on-market supply limiting the extent of the correction. For more, see our latest Housing Pulse.

Offshore, there was plenty of data to dissect.
In the US, inflation eased in August despite ongoing strength in household demand and higher energy
prices. Headline PCE inflation rose 0.3%mth and 3.4%yr, while core PCE increased 0.2%mth and
3.0%yr. Encouragingly for the FOMC, the share of PCE components rising by more than 3% over the
past year, a measure frequently cited by Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh, declined to 45% from 49%,
suggesting some easing in broad-based inflation pressures. At the same time, household spending

remained robust, increasing 0.9%mth, equivalent to around 0.6%mth in real terms. That said, personal
income growth was soft, rising 0.2% in the month, signalling households opted to draw down savings.

### Page 3

The ISM manufacturing survey was stronger than the headline suggested. While the index edged down
0.1pt to 54.5, manufacturing has now expanded for nine consecutive months. There were some signs
of capacity constraints emerging, with production softened in the month but backlogs up 4.6pts to 56.4,
suggesting that firms struggled to keep pace with demand. New orders rose 1.6pts to 55.3, primarily
due to domestic demand as new export orders eased, backlogs increased. Firms responded by lifting

employment, 1.5pts to 52.7. Against this backdrop manufacturers saw a renewed acceleration in cost
pressures, with the prices paid index jumping 6.8pts to 77.9. Firms cited higher steel and aluminium
prices, elevated energy costs, rising freight charges and ongoing semiconductor shortages, with every
major industry reporting higher input costs during the month.
The past week’s data will have done nothing to bridge the clear divide across the FOMC, as evident in

this week’s busy Fedspeak. Some participants argued for further rate hikes, citing strong inflation,
diminished supply capacity and policy that is not yet restrictive. Others counselled patience, pointing to
progress on inflation and that September’s hike buys time to assess upcoming data. The one thing
both agree on is that inflation will be the primary determinant of the timing and extent of further rate
hikes. Our view remains that one further hike is likely this cycle to bring policy closer to neutral.

In China, policymakers announced another round of targeted support measures aimed at stabilising
growth and supporting the struggling property sector. The PBOC lowered rates on its Pledged
Supplementary Lending facility by 25bps to 1.5% and expanded quotas for targeted lending programs.
Authorities also introduced subsidies for eligible first-home buyers. The subsidy applies to homes with
a floor area of 120m² or less and a value below CNY1.5mn, with support capped at 1ppt of mortgage

costs, up to CNY10,000 and for a maximum term of five years. The measures complement reforms
announced in August to improve developer financing conditions and support housing completion.
However, weak credit demand and confidence remains a significant constraint, suggesting policy
transmission is still limited.
The September PMI data pointed to a modest improvement in activity, although the details were less
convincing. Manufacturing returned to expansionary territory, rising 0.9pts to 50.1, supported by

stronger production and new orders. Much of the improvement, however, reflected a rebound in prices,
with the raw materials price index rising 7.6pts and producer prices increasing 6.2pts. Labour market
conditions remained weak, with the employment index falling a further 0.6pts and remaining below 50
for almost four years. Conditions in the non-manufacturing sector also improved, with the services PMI
lifting from 49.0 to 50.2, driven largely by stronger construction activity. Input and output prices moved

higher, selling prices returned back above 50, export orders improved to 48.5 and employment
increased 0.5pts, although at 45.9 it remains firmly in contractionary territory. Overall, the surveys
suggest activity is stabilising and policy support is gaining some traction, but weak labour demand,
subdued credit growth and soft domestic demand indicate the recovery remains fragile.
In Japan, the September Tankan survey delivered another constructive signal on the economy and

reinforced the case for further policy normalisation. Business conditions improved by 2pts for large
manufacturers and 5pts more broadly, while profitability indicators strengthened and capital
expenditure intentions remained elevated. Large manufacturers expect fixed investment to increase by
11.6% in FY2026, while large non-manufacturers expect investment growth of 11.2%, pointing to

### Page 4

Related articles
continued confidence in the medium-term outlook.
Capacity indicators improved further across both manufacturing and non-manufacturing sectors, with
measures of excess production capacity becoming less negative and indicating that demand remains
sufficiently strong to keep pressure on existing capacity. Labour shortages also intensified, with
employment conditions becoming more negative across most industries and firm sizes, pointing to a

progressively tighter labour market. Inflation expectations remained anchored around 2%, providing
further evidence that Japan is moving towards a more sustainable inflation environment. Overall, the
survey was consistent with an economy operating close to capacity and supports the view that a
December rate hike remains a realistic possibility.

Document 269: CoreWeave资金缺口有多大？高盛做了详细测算

Source PDF: all report pdf/CoreWeave资金缺口有多大？高盛做了详细测算.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛测算， CoreWeave 消化 1290 亿美元积压订单需⾼达 600 亿美元资本⽀出，但客⼾预付
款（占合同价值 20% ）是核⼼减压阀 —— 引⼊预付款后，实际新增债务可压缩⾄ 230 亿美
元；若激活全部 4.2GW 电⼒储备⽀撑 2630 亿潜在订单，则需 380 亿美元新债。市场对融资
⻛险过度悲观， 12 个⽉⽬标价 106 美元，较当前股价有 71.5% 的上⾏空间。
摘要：
对于算⼒基础设施巨头 CoreWeave （ CRWV ）的投资者⽽⾔，眼下最核⼼的博弈点已经从 “ 需求
有多⼤ ” 转向了 “ 钱从哪⾥来 ” 。
据追⻛交易台消息，⾼盛在 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇的研报中直击痛点，对 CoreWeave 的资⾦缺⼝进⾏了极其
硬核的数学测算。核⼼结论是：为了消化截⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉⾼达 1290 亿美元的庞⼤积压订单，
CoreWeave ⾯临约 600 亿美元的资本⽀出（ Capex ）墙。
然⽽，⾼盛认为，投资者⽆需过度恐慌。通过巧妙利⽤客⼾预付款（占⽐约 20% ）， CoreWeave

有望将实际需要筹集的新增债务规模⼤幅压缩⾄ 200 亿⾄ 230 亿美元。如果进⼀步将其已锁定的
4.2GW 电⼒储备全部转化为产能，以⽀撑未来 2600 亿美元的潜在订单，公司将需要约380 亿美
元的新增债务。
⾼盛维持对该股的“ 买⼊ ” 评级，⽬标价 106 美元，意味着较当前 61.82 美元的股价有⾼达 71.5%
的上涨空间，并指出 CoreWeave 的估值兑现将完全取决于其在债务融资和客⼾预付款之间的杠
杆平衡能⼒。
消化现有 1290 亿美元订单：最⾼⾯临 600 亿美元资本⽀出
要理解 CoreWeave 的资⾦压⼒，⾸先要看其现有的订单规模。⾼盛测算，在没有任何客⼾预付
款的极端基准情景下（假设 GPU 使⽤寿命为 4.5 年），CoreWeave 需要约 600 亿美元的资本⽀出
来消化⽬前 1290 亿美元的积压订单。
这笔巨款如何筹集？⾼盛给出了详细的拆解：
债务融资上限：基于 CoreWeave 现有的延迟提款定期贷款（ DDTL ）结构，资本⽀出的 80%

可以通过债务融资，即最⾼ 480 亿美元。扣除已经承诺但尚未提取的约110 亿美元 DDTL，公
司还需要筹集370 亿美元的新增债务。
现⾦缺⼝：剩余的 20% （约 120 亿美元）必须由现⾦（ SPV 出资）填补。⾼盛计算得出，
CoreWeave ⽬前的可⽤现⾦总额约为 104 亿美元（包括 2026 年⼆季度的 55 亿美元现⾦、扣除
30 亿美元运营⽀出缓冲、 9 ⽉可转债净收益、 20 亿美元循环信贷，以及按 64 美元计算的 3500
万股 ATM 股票发⾏）。
最终缺⼝：这意味着在⽆预付款情景下，公司仍有16 亿美元的直接现⾦缺⼝，需要通过客⼾
预付款、供应商融资或扣除债务服务后的运营现⾦流来填补。
破局关键：客⼾预付款将新发债务⼤幅降⾄ 230 亿美元
⾼盛指出， CoreWeave 的商业模式中隐藏着⼀个巨⼤的减压阀 ——客⼾预付款。⽬前，公司
70% 的合同都包含客⼾预付款条款，平均占总合同价值（ TCV ）的 15%-25%。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

当⾼盛将20% 的客⼾预付款纳⼊模型后，资⾦压⼒骤降：
在匹配合同期限（ 4.5 年 GPU 使⽤寿命）的前提下，所需的新增债务规模将⼤幅下降⾄约 230
亿美元。
在这种情景下，债务对贷款价值⽐（ Debt to loan value ）将降⾄ 50%-75% 的区间，这意味着
CoreWeave 完全可以选择低于 80% 的债务杠杆率。
更乐观的假设： 如果假设 GPU 的有效使⽤寿命为 6 年（即存在正向剩余价值），在包含预付款
的情况下，新增债务规模甚⾄可以进⼀步降⾄ 195 亿美元。
值得注意的是，过去 6 个⽉随着公司规模扩⼤和资本成本降低，预付款⽐例有所下降；但⾼盛认
为，在债务融资能⻅度较低的环境下， CoreWeave 完全有能⼒重新提⾼这⼀⽐例。
终极扩张：激活 4.2GW 电⼒储备需 380 亿美元新债

### Page 3

除了现有的 1290 亿美元订单，市场的更⼤想象空间在于 CoreWeave 庞⼤的电⼒储备。截⾄ 2026
年 8 ⽉，公司已锁定 4.2GW 的电⼒容量（相⽐之下， 2026 年⼆季度的活跃容量仅为 1.5GW ）。
⾼盛测算，如果要将这 4.2GW 的电⼒全部转化为上线产能：
这将能够⽀撑⾼达约 2630 亿美元的潜在积压订单（⼏乎是 8 ⽉份 1290 亿美元订单的两倍）。
在包含 20% 客⼾预付款的假设下，CoreWeave 需要筹集约 380 亿美元的新增债务来完成这⼀
宏⼤的扩张计划。
估值与⽬标价： 71.5% 的上⾏空间从何⽽来？
基于上述测算，⾼盛给出了106 美元的 12 个⽉⽬标价，该估值基于未来第 5 ⾄第 8 季度（ Q5-Q8 ）
预期息税前利润（ EBIT ）的 18 倍乘数。当前股价 61.82 美元对应的潜在上⾏空间为71.5%。
值得注意的是， CoreWeave 股价⾃年初以来已⼤幅回调：
这⼀背景下，⾼盛的买⼊评级和 106 美元⽬标价，本质上是在押注：市场对融资⻛险的定价已经

过度悲观，⽽ CoreWeave 的订单积压和电⼒资产构成了⾜够强⼤的基本⾯⽀撑。
然⽽，⾼盛也明确提⽰了投资者必须警惕的四⼤核⼼⻛险：
融资受阻：筹集数百亿美元债务融资的过程中可能遇到障碍。
产能爬坡延迟：数据中⼼产能上线的执⾏⻛险。
时间错配与通胀：签订合同的定价时间与实际产能上线时的成本通胀之间可能存在时间错
配。
过去 3 个⽉：下跌 22.3% ，跑输标普 500 指数 24.4 个百分点；过去 6 个⽉：下跌 46.3% ，跑输标普
500 指数 50.8 个百分点；过去 12 个⽉：下跌 50.6% ，跑输标普 500 指数 56.8 个百分点。

### Page 4

供需动态恶化：算⼒市场供需关系逆转，导致新合同或续约合同的定价能⼒下降。
CoreWeave 的投资逻辑⾼度依赖于债务资本市场的持续开放和 AI 算⼒需求的持续旺盛。⼀旦融
资环境收紧或 AI 需求预期出现逆转，公司的⾼杠杆结构将⾯临严峻考验。⾼盛的测算表明，在最
乐观的情景下（含客⼾预付款 +6 年 GPU 寿命），新增债务需求约 195 亿美元，仍是⼀个需要在资
本市场完成的庞⼤融资任务。

Document 418: “DeepSeek才是真正的OpenAI”

Source PDF: all report pdf/“DeepSeek才是真正的OpenAI”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 的名字⾥虽然带着 “ 开放 ” ，但其核⼼模型⻓期采取闭源策略，反倒是来⾃中国的
DeepSeek 正⽤实际⾏动践⾏ “ 开放 ” ⼀词，可能是对开源⼤语⾔模型⽣态贡献最⼤的公司之
⼀。
摘要：
在美国⼈⼯智能（ AI ）企业专注价格昂贵的闭源模型之际， DeepSeek 将⾃⼰的技术 “ 家底 ” 向外
界开放，赢得海外⽹友称赞。
10 ⽉ 20 ⽇， DeepSeek 宣布开源内部⽣产级推理框架 DeepInfer 。
该框架是 DeepSeek 在⾯临美国尖端芯⽚出⼝限制下，确保⼤模型紧追美国顶尖 AI 模型的核⼼。
推理框架负责管理模型如何使⽤计算资源来处理⽤⼾请求，其设计会影响延迟、吞吐量以及运
⾏模型所需的硬件，因此是影响 AI 运营成本的重要因素。
美国彭博社 21 ⽇指出，推理技术通常被 AI 公司视为核⼼竞争⼒，较少对外公开，⽽ DeepSeek 此
次公开推理框架，让外界得以⼀窥其是如何在压低运⾏成本的同时，确保 AI 模型性能的。
即使是同⼀款 AI 模型，实际运⾏成本也可能因所采⽤的软件不同⽽存在较⼤差异。更加⾼效的软

件能够降低每次请求所需的计算资源，并有望进⼀步减少能源消耗。
DeepInfer 整合了分布式推理、投机解码等多项技术，能够让 AI ⼯作负载分配到不同硬件上，从
⽽让模型在更短时间内⽣成更多输出，并进⼀步挖掘芯⽚的性能。⽬前，该框架已兼容英伟达
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

SM100 GPU 和华为昇腾 950 NPU 。
这其中不少的底层技术已被业界⼴泛采⽤，但 DeepSeek 针对⾃⾝模型进⾏了专⻔的优化，使这
些技术能够更加⾼效地协同运⾏。
美媒称，对于 AI 托管服务商⽽⾔，更低的推理成本意味着更⼤的利润空间，并有可能进⼀步转化
为更低的 AI 服务价格。对于企业⽤⼾来说，如果选择在⾃有基础设施上运⾏ DeepSeek 模型，
DeepInfer 也可能帮助其降低算⼒成本。
不仅如此， DeepSeek 开源 DeepInfer 后，其他开发者也可以研究其中的技术⽅案，并将部分⽅
法应⽤到⾃⼰的模型推理系统中。
在 DeepSeek 宣布开源 DeepInfer 的帖⼦下⽅，美国加州⼀家专注于⼤语⾔模型与 AI 开源优化、
本地训练和推理的公司 Unsloth AI 公开感谢 DeepSeek 称，其已经借助 Dynamic GGUFs ，让
DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017 能够在配备 128GB 内存的设备上实现本地运⾏。
评论区还出现了⼤量海外开发者和 AI 从业者的积极反馈。⼀名来⾃印度的社交媒体博主直接

⽤ “Real OPEN AI” （真正的开放 AI ）评价 DeepSeek，并配上⼀段调侃 OpenAI ⾸席执⾏官⼭姆 ·
奥尔特曼的视频。
欧洲 AI 从业者也⽤漫画嘲讽美国 AI 公司的闭源模型仅限特定 “ 朋友圈 ” ，与之相对的是中国公司通
过降低成本，让 AI 模型更普惠。

Document 424: 「社外取締役が議長」は何を変えるのか ～プライム市場で118社、大型企業で選任進む～

Source PDF: all report pdf/「社外取締役が議長」は何を変えるのか ～プライム市場で118社、大型企業で選任進む～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年 10 月 8 日

1/4 
「社外取締役が議長」は何を変えるのか 
～プライム市場で 118 社、大型企業で選任進む～ 
 資産運用調査部 シニア研究員  河谷 善夫

1．社外取締役の取締役会議長選任は緩やかに拡大 
筆者は、昨年8 月中旬時点でのプライム市場上場会社のうち、社外取締役を取締役会議長に選
任している会社について分析したレポート（河谷、2025）を公表し、2022 年 7 月上旬時点より、
その数が増加していることを示した。本稿では、2026 年9 月末の時点でのプライム市場における
社外取締役の取締役会議長選任の状況をフォローアップした上で、最後に社外取締役の取締役会
議長選任の意味について考える。

資料 1 社外取締役の取締役会議長選任会社数の推移

（出所）東証公表資料を基に第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

資料 1 のとおり、2025 年 8 月から2026 年 9 月にかけて会社数は9 社増加し、プライム市場での
比率は 6.7％から7.6％と0.9％ポイント上昇した。2022 年以降、年平均で概ね1％ポイントの上
昇ペースが続いている。このグラフだけでは見えないが、2025 年8 月から2026 年9 月にかけて継
続してプライム市場に上場している会社では17 社が社外取締役を新たに取締役会議長に選任して
おり（注1）、新規選任の動きは続いている。 
また、118 名中 117 名が独立社外取締役であり、社外取締役議長はほぼすべて独立社外取締役で
ある（注2）。

### Page 2

2/4 
2．特定の機関設計に限らず普及、ただし社外取締役議長はなお少数 
資料 2 は、会社の機関設計別の2022 年、2025 年、2026 年のプライム市場での社外取締役の取
締役会議長選任会社数と割合を示したものである。

資料 2 機関設計別の社外取締役の取締役会議長選任状況（プライム市場）

（出所）資料 1 と同じ

2026 年では、社外取締役を取締役会議長に選任している会社は、監査役設置会社で5.3％、監
査等委員会設置会社で6.0％、指名委員会等設置会社で42.7％である。指名委員会等設置会社の
割合は依然として高いが、2022 年からの増加状況をみると、社外取締役を取締役会議長とする動
きは、指名委員会等設置会社以外にも徐々に広まっており、昨年のレポートで確認した傾向は続
いているといえる。 
なお、これも資料で示していないが、取締役の過半を独立社外取締役とする会社でも、社外取
締役を取締役会議長とする会社は約2 割にとどまり、2025 年 8 月から2026 年 9 月にかけて大きな
変化はなかった（注3）。

3．社長を議長とする会社の減少と、大型企業で先行する社外取締役議長選任 
資料 3 では、2022 年、2025 年、2026 年のプライム市場での取締役会議長の属性の変化をグラフ
として示した。

資料 3 取締役会議長の属性の変化（プライム市場）

（注）「その他の取締役等」には、会長・社長以外の執行役を兼任する取締役を含む。 
（出所）資料 1 と同じ

### Page 3

3/4 
2022 年からの変化をみると、社長を取締役会議長とする会社の割合は71.4％から65.6％へ
5.8％ポイント低下する一方、社外取締役を議長とする会社の割合は、3.6％から 7.6％へ4.0％ポ
イント上昇し、会長を議長とする会社の割合も1.5％ポイント上昇している。このような社長を取
締役会議長とする会社の割合の低下、社外取締役を議長とする会社の割合の上昇は、取締役会議
長と社長を分離するという、より大きな流れの一部とみることができるだろう。 
次に、どのような会社で社外取締役の取締役会議長選任が進んでいるのかをみるため、2026 年
9 月末時点のプライム市場上場会社について、時価総額ランク別の選任割合を示した（資料4）。

資料 4 時価総額ランク別社外取締役議長選任割合（プライム市場・2026 年 9 月末）

（出所）Bloomberg、東証公表資料データを基に第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

資料 4 からは、時価総額の大きい企業ほど、社外取締役の取締役会議長選任が進んでいるとい
える。なお、社外取締役を取締役会議長に選任している会社の社数の割合は7.6％だが、時価総額
では 19％を占める。また、社外取締役が議長となっている会社の39％が時価総額１兆円以上の会
社である。時価総額が大きい企業ほど選任率が高く、大型企業での社外取締役の取締役会議長選
任が相対的に進んでいる。

4．最後に 
ここまで、社外取締役の取締役会議長選任状況について確認してきた。このような社外取締役
の取締役会議長選任はコーポレートガバナンス上、どのような意味を持つのか、最後に私見を述
べたい。 
とりわけ独立社外取締役が議長を務める場合には、独立した立場から取締役会の議論を促し、
監督機能を含む取締役会の実効性向上につながることが期待される。そのためには、経営陣との
適切な連絡・調整や十分な情報提供が重要となる。今回のコーポレートガバナンス・コード改訂
でも、議長や独立社外取締役を支える取締役会事務局（コーポレートセクレタリー等）の機能強

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
化の重要性が示されている。社外取締役を議長に選任する企業には、選任によってどのような効
果を目指すのかを明確にし、それに即した支援体制を整えることが重要であろう。

【注釈】 
1) 2025 年8 月中旬のプライム市場上場会社（1,618 社）のうち、社外取締役を取締役会議長に選
任している会社数の異同については次のとおり。2026 年 9 月末までの間にプライム市場から退
出した会社（95 社）で社外取締役を取締役会議長に選任していた7 社と、継続上場会社（1,523
社）で社外取締役以外を取締役会議長にした会社1 社の計8 社が減少。継続上場会社のうち17 社
が新規に社外取締役を取締役会議長に選任したので、差し引き9 社の増加となった。新規にプライ
ム市場に上場した会社（20 社）には、社外取締役を取締役会議長に選任していた会社はなかった。 
2) なお、2026 年 9 月末でプライム市場上場会社の全社外取締役数は、6,986 名であり、そのうち
6,793 名（97.2％）が独立社外取締役である。ほとんどの社外取締役は独立社外取締役である
といってよい状況となっている。 
3) 一方、取締役の過半数を独立社外取締役とする会社が着実に増加していることは、河谷
（2026）で示したとおりである。

先進製程擴張為營運成長基礎 
投資與評價 
1. 光阻劑、矽晶圓、光罩等半導體核心材料需求持續強勁，另環保工程業
務受惠於大型專案認列，2Q26 營收優於我們先前預期。 
2. 綜觀 7~9 月營收皆維持高檔，研究部推估主要受惠於晶圓代工業產能
持續擴張，且另有漲價挹注營收表現，3Q26 營收為 236.7 億元。 
3. 半導體主力產品及新品需求皆見成長，帶動半導體材料業務持續維持季
增，研究部上調 FY26 光阻劑營收全年年增率達 30%以上。 
4. 研究部上調 FY26/FY27 營收獲利預估，預估 FY26/FY27 EPS 分別為 
30.72/32.34 元。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價調升至 700 元，以
21x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
半導體材料業務仍為今明年成長主旋律 
半導體主力產品及新品需求皆見成長，帶動半導體材料業務持續維持季增。
研究部上調 FY26 光阻劑營收全年年增率達 30%以上。矽晶圓市場需求情況
逐漸轉好 。 藍寶石基板 、 散熱材料等新品亦開始於1H26 成長力道逐漸顯著 ，
使其他電子材料 1H26 年增率達 30%以上。研究部預期產品調漲效應將延續
至 4Q26， 帶動公司FY26 整體營運逐季走高 ，FY27 營運表現亦將優於 FY26。 
工程業務 2H26 進入工程傳統認列旺季 
大型案件有所斬獲，環保工程在手訂單約近 200 億水平。隨下半年進入工
程傳統認列旺季，研究部預期營收將逐季走高，預估 FY26 全年工程業務營
收將年增 20%以上。崇越持續進行海外布局，目前東南亞案件持續進行
中，亦逐漸朝美國 AI 領域工程拓展，今年將陸續於多國設立據點，奠定工
程業務及半導體材料業務海外發展基礎。 
先進製程擴張為營運成長基礎 
信越於先進製程穩固的市佔率奠定崇越營運成長基礎。受惠先進製程產能持
續釋出且 UTR 維持高檔，加上成熟製程於 2H26 復甦趨勢確立，晶圓代工
產業動能明確。在此背景下，崇越憑藉於光阻劑與矽晶圓的穩固市占，並積
極布局先進封裝材料，奠定產業龍頭地位；此外，工程業務營收認列持續改
善，疊加在手訂單持續維持高水平，挹注營運動能。考量半導體材料業務動
能提升，研究部上調 FY26/FY27 營收獲利預估。

訪談內容 
半導體核心材料需求強勁、環工業務受惠於大型專案認列挹注 2Q26 營收表現 
光阻劑、矽晶圓、光罩等半導體核心材料需求持續強勁，另環保工程業務受惠於大型專案認列，
營收呈現季增，使 2Q26 營收達 208.3 億元(YoY+22.6%/QoQ +12.3%)，創下歷史新高，亦優
於 我 們 先 前 預 期 的 194.1 億元。 產 品 組 合 轉 好帶動 毛利率 持 續 維 持 高 檔 ， 為13.5% 
(YoY+1.8ppts/ QoQ-0.1ppts)，符合我們先前預期的 13.3%。EPS 創下歷史新高，達 7.92 元
(YoY+65.5%/QoQ+15.8%)，優於研究部原先預期的 6.58 元。 
綜觀 7~9 月營收皆維持高檔，研究部推估主要受惠於晶圓代工業產能持續擴張，且另有漲價挹
注營收表現， 3Q26 營收為 236.7 億元 (YoY+34.9%/QoQ+13.6%)；預估毛利率將 持穩於
13.3%(QoQ -0.2ppts/ YoY +1.6ppts) ， 產 品 組 合 維 持 穩 定；預期 EPS 為 8.42 元
(YoY+46.7%/QoQ+6.4%)。

半導體材料業務仍為今明年成長主旋律 
半導體主力產品及新品需求皆見成長，帶動 半導體材料業務持續維持季增。 光阻劑業務營收於
1H26 年增率達 30%以上，研究部上調 FY26 光阻劑營收全年年增率達 30%以上。矽晶圓市場需
求情況逐漸轉好，受惠於先進製程及記憶體廠 UTR 平均達 95~100%，出貨價格持續依 LTA 進度
調整。除光阻劑、矽晶圓等隨先進製程產能開出，藍寶石基板、散熱材料及 Underfill 等新品亦開
始於 1H26 成長力道逐漸顯著，使其他電子材料 1H26 年增率達 30%以上。除需求成長外，在油
價持續 維持高檔下，日本信越開始針對部分產品進行漲價，依據產品類別不同，漲價幅度約
15~20%，其中例如 FOSB 受成本影響較大且需求高，售價可能提升將更為顯著，研究部預期半
導體材料產品調漲效應將延續至 4Q26，帶動公司 FY26 整體營運逐季走高，FY27 營運表現亦將
優於 FY26。

工程業務 2H26 進入工程傳統認列旺季 
大型案件有所斬獲，環保工程在手訂單約近 200 億水平，分布於台灣、中國、新加坡、越南、馬來
西亞各區域。2Q26 工程業務營收顯著回穩，顯示在手訂單已逐步轉化為實際營收。隨下半年進入
工程傳統認列旺季 ， 研究部預期營收將逐季走高 ， 預估FY26 全年工程業務營收將年增 20%以上 。
崇越持續進行海外布局，目前東南亞案件持續進行中，亦逐漸朝美國 AI 領域工程拓展，今年將陸
續於多國設立據點，奠定工程業務及半導體材料業務海外發展基礎。

### Page 3

先進製程擴張為營運成長基礎 
信越於先進製程穩固的市佔率奠定崇越營運成長基礎。受惠先進製程產能持續釋出且 UTR 維持
高檔，加上成熟製程於 2H26 復甦趨勢確立，晶圓代工產業動能明確。在此背景下，崇越憑藉於
光阻劑與矽晶圓的穩固市占，並積極布局先進封裝材料，奠定產業龍頭地位；此外，工程業務營
收認列持續改善，疊加在手訂單持續維持高水平，挹注營運動能。考量半導體材料業務動能提升 ，
研究部上調 FY26/FY27 營 收 獲 利 預 估 ， 預 估FY26/27 營收分別為 865.0/964.7 億元
(YoY+28.0%/11.5%)，毛利率分別達 13.4%/13.3%，FY26/FY27 EPS 分別為 30.72/32.34 元
(YoY+42.6%/5.3%)。評價方面，崇越目前交易於 19.4/18.4 倍之今明年獲利預估，研究部維持
買進投資建議，目標價調升至 700 元，以 21x FY27 獲利預估。

公司簡介 
崇越科技成立於 1990 年，為半導體製程材料代理通路商，主要業務包含 1)半導體相關材料：在
中國及台灣獨家代理日廠信越化學(4063 JP)的矽晶圓/光阻劑/其他製程材料 ， 崇越與信越合作關
係緊密，先後合資成立信越光電、台灣信越半導體股份有限公司、崇越石英等公司。另外崇越亦
代理 FUJIMI(5384 JP)的研磨液。2) 光電相關材料：包含LED/LCD/其他電子材料等。3) 環保工
程：以廠務廢水處理為主，另有機電工程、水回收等廠務工程，主要來自蘇州工程及台灣建越。
4) 綠色能源：太陽能系統工程整合服務等。 
從客戶面來看，半導體相關材料業務中，台積電(2330 TT)為其最大客戶，占比營收約 20%，另
有如中芯國際、聯電、美光等客戶。環保工程業務客戶則有記憶體、面板及其他電子下游產業。

### Page 4

ESG 政策 
崇越在 MSCI ESG 評鑑 BB，台灣永續評鑑中評級為 AA，公司治理評鑑前 20％。另外崇越為富
邦公司治理 ETF(00692)成分股，其中於「經濟面」表現尤其突出，特別是股東權益、董事會結構
與運作、風險及危機管理。 
 利害關係人溝通與重大議題管理 
崇越科技重視與利害關係人溝通 ， 日常各項業務均保持與利害關係人良好溝通與互動 ， 依循2021
年改版之 GRI 通用準則 ， 依永續性脈絡 、 重大性 、 完整性及利害關係人包容性 ， 進行鑑別 、 排序 、
確認與檢視等之重大議題管理流程。 
 風險管理 
崇越科技考量更多異常事件應變措施 ， 提早整合公司發展政策 ， 可於危機發生時 ， 成為存續企業 。
成立資訊安全委員會，掌管營運所需之資訊科技相關事項。此外，2023 年亦成立資安室，設置
資訊安全專責主管與資安人員 ， 負責資訊安全相關工作與落實 。 已規劃於2024 年導入 ISO27001
資訊安全管理系統，預計 2025 取證認證。

英文評等 中文評等 潛在報酬率

BUY 買進 介於+15% ~ +30%

SELL 賣出 < -15%

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

調査レポート

コモディティ・レポート（2026 年 7～9 月）

調査部 主任研究員 芥田 知至  
1. コモディティ市況全般：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数は、2023 年 5 月 31 日に 2022
年 1 月以来の安値をつけた後、2026 年 3～9 月には大幅に上昇して 9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値を
つけた。その後、10 月にかけてやや下落した。コモディティ相場は、中東情勢や米金融政策の行方を巡って、引
き続き、不安定な推移が見こまれる。

2. エネルギー市況：足元はブレントが一時 110ドル、WTI が 100 ドル前後 
国際指標のブレント原油は、2026 年 4 月 30 日に 126.41 ドルと 2022 年 3 月以来の高値をつけた。足元は
110 ドル前後で不安定な動きとなっている。米国とイランは戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開に向けた動きは停滞し
たままであり、緊迫する中東情勢を受けて原油は高止まりが見こまれる。

3. ベースメタル市況：銅は 9 月上旬に 14,875 ドルの史上最高値 
銅相場は、2026 年 9 月 10 日に 14,875 ドルと史上最高値を記録し、足元は 14,000 ドル台半ばを中心に推
移している。銅は、原油高や景気悪化の影響が懸念される一方で、 AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中
心に需要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、高値圏での一進一退が見こまれる。

4. 貴金属市況：金は 8 月中旬に 4,700 ドル近くまで持ち直すも足元は 4,100 ドル前後 
金は、2026 年 1 月 29 日に 5,594.82 ドルと史上最高値を記録した後、6 月 30 日には 3,942.99 ドルまで下
落し、足元は 4,100 ドル前後を中心に推移している。米利上げが続くとの観測が有力な中、中東情勢も安定化に
はまだ遠く、原油高が意識されて、金は買われにくい状況が続きやすいとみられる。

原油・銅・金相場の騰落率（2026 年 9 月末の 2026 年 6 月末との対比）

1. コモディティ市況全般の概況：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数（終値ベース）は、2022 年 6 月
9 日に 2011 年 9 月以来の高値をつけた後、2023 年 5 月 31 日には 2022 年 1 月以来の安値をつけた。2026
年 3～9 月に大幅に上昇し、9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値をつけた。10 月にかけてやや下落した。 
マクロ経済環境をみると、フーシ派とサウジアラビアによる攻撃の応酬が激しさを増すなど中東の混乱に広がり
がみられ、米国とイランも戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開へ向けた動きは停滞したままである。原油は緊迫する中
東情勢を受けて上昇傾向で推移した。金は、原油高などを受けた根強い米利上げ観測を背景に上値が重い状
況が続いた。銅は、原油高や景気悪化が懸念される一方で、 AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中心に需
要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、史上最高値圏で一進一退となった。中東情勢や米金融政策を巡って、コモディテ
ィ相場は不安定な推移が続くと見こまれる。
